2. The band feature

Protocol types — /Users/benjaminjordan/projects/thegoldenmule/aibleton/packages/protocol/src/bands.ts

// :7
export const GENRES = ["funk", "jazz", "rock", "metal", "house", "hiphop", "ambient", "reggae"] as const;
export const GenreSchema = z.enum(GENRES);
export type Genre = z.infer&lt;typeof GenreSchema&gt;;

// :12  — suggested vocabulary only; Part.role is free text
export const ROLES = ["drums","percussion","bass","guitar","keys","synth","horns","strings","vocals","fx"] as const;
export type Role = (typeof ROLES)[number];

// :27
export const PartIdSchema = z.string()
  .regex(/^[a-z0-9][a-z0-9-]{0,31}$/, "part id must be a lowercase slug, e.g. \"keys-rhodes\"");

// :36
export const BandPartSchema = z.object({
  id: PartIdSchema,
  /** Free text, but usually one of ROLES. */
  role: z.string().min(1),
  /** What this player is called on the stage plot, e.g. "Rhodes" or "rhythm guitar". */
  name: z.string().min(1),
  /** Prompt text handed to Splice when this part is bound to material. */
  brief: z.string().min(1),
  /** Reserved for a later mix-prominence input. Nothing reads it yet. */
  emphasis: z.number().min(0).max(1).optional(),
});
export type BandPart = z.infer&lt;typeof BandPartSchema&gt;;

// :49
const BandShape = z.object({
  id: z.string().min(1),
  name: z.string().min(1),
  /** Ordered: this is the track order in Ableton. */
  parts: z.array(BandPartSchema).min(1),
  /** Free-form tags. `genre` is a conventional key; nothing here is required. */
  metadata: z.record(z.string(), z.string()).default({}),
  createdAt: z.number(),
});

// :64 — superRefine rejects duplicate part ids
export const BandSchema = BandShape.superRefine(...);
export type Band = z.infer&lt;typeof BandSchema&gt;;
export type BandDraft = z.input&lt;typeof BandSchema&gt;;   // :79 metadata optional

Helpers: bandRoles(band) → distinct roles in part order (:82), bandGenre(band) → band.metadata.genre ?? null (:94).

Key doc comment (:59): "Who plays in a song. Bands are the space axis; templates are the time axis. A composition is a template crossed with a band: parts become tracks, sections become scenes." — this is the intended data model.

Store — .../mate/src/core/bands.ts (32 lines, thin)

isValidBandId(id) delegates to isValidDocumentId (document-store.ts:6 — /^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{1,64}$/). BandStore extends DocumentStore&lt;Band&gt; with schema: BandSchema, idOf: b =&gt; b.id, kind: "band", sortKey: b =&gt; b.createdAt (list is newest-first). The store never reads the clock.

Generator — .../mate/src/core/band-generator.ts (703 lines, mostly the RECIPES data table)

// :11
export interface BandRecipe {
  /** Always staffed, in this order — this is the track order in Ableton. A role may repeat (metal has two guitars). */
  core: Role[];
  /** Drawn by weight, without replacement, until `size` is met. A role may repeat a core role. */
  optional: { role: Role; weight: number }[];
  /** Display names per role, drawn without replacement so duplicate roles read differently. */
  names: Partial&lt;Record&lt;Role, string[]&gt;&gt;;
  /** Splice prompt text per role, paired by index with `names`. */
  briefs: Partial&lt;Record&lt;Role, string[]&gt;&gt;;
  /** How many leading `names` entries a *core* slot may be drawn from, per role. */
  anchors?: Partial&lt;Record&lt;Role, number&gt;&gt;;
}

// :30
export interface GenerateBandOptions {
  seed: number;      // any integer; same seed + options =&gt; identical band
  genre?: Genre;     // omit to let the seed pick from GENRES
  size?: number;     // total parts, clamped to the recipe's range
}

// :94
export const RECIPES: Record&lt;Genre, BandRecipe&gt; = { funk: {...}, jazz: {...}, ... };

// :633
export function generateBand(opts: GenerateBandOptions):
  { parts: BandPart[]; metadata: Record&lt;string, string&gt; }

RNG / seeding. mulberry32(seed) at band-generator.ts:43 — pure, no Math.random(), a = (seed|0)&gt;&gt;&gt;0 so negative and >2³² seeds are folded deterministically. This function is duplicated verbatim at .../mate/src/core/generator.ts:33; a shared rng.ts doesn't exist yet.

Draw order inside generateBand (this order is the contract — changing it reshuffles every existing seed): 1. genre = GENRES[floor(rng()*len)] — always drawn, even when opts.genre is supplied (:642). 2. seededSize = min + round(((rng()+rng())/2) * optional.length) — always drawn too; averaging two rolls gives a triangular pull toward mid-sized bands (:650). Then size = clamp(opts.size ?? seededSize, min, max) where min = core.length, max = min + optional.length. This is why {seed, size: n} is byte-identical to {seed} when the seed itself picked n — tested at band-generator.test.ts:87. 3. roles = [...core, ...pickOptional(optional, size-min, rng)] — weighted, without replacement (pickOptional at :593). 4. Per role, a name is drawn from the role's pool without replacement. Archetype-first (the e6d647b commit): a core slot only sees window = min(pool.length, max(anchors[role] ?? 0, coreMultiplicity[role])) head entries; optionals see the whole pool (:671-672). When the window has no slack (window &lt;= coreCount), names are taken in pool order rather than randomly (inOrder, :679), so metal's two core guitars are always ["down-tuned rhythm guitar", "doubled rhythm guitar"] in that order. 5. Brief is briefPool[index % briefPool.length] — parallel to names, so a clav never gets a drawbar brief. 6. id = partId(role, name, taken) → slugify(\${role}-${name}`), clipped to 32 chars without a trailing dash, numeric suffix on collision (:79`).

Pool exhaustion fallback (:688) cycles names with a numeric suffix; a test asserts pools are deep enough that it never fires in normal generation.

Throws on non-integer seed, size &lt; 1/non-integer, unknown genre. Returns parts + metadata: { genre } only — id, name, createdAt and emphasis are the caller's job.

Routes — .../mate/src/api/routes/bands.ts

bandRoutes({ bands: BandStore, now: () =&gt; number }). now is injected specifically so tests drive createdAt and the default seed from a ManualClock. POST /bands/generate (:61): parses GenerateBandRequestSchema, defaults seed = deps.now(), calls generateBand, builds a draft with id: newId("band") and name: opts.name ?? \${genre} band ${seed}`, re-validates withBandSchema(500 on failure), and returns it **unsaved** —POST /bands` persists.

Request/response schemas (packages/protocol/src/api.ts:62-87):

export const GenerateBandRequestSchema = z.object({
  seed: z.number().int().optional(),
  genre: GenreSchema.optional(),
  /** Total parts. Clamped to what the genre's recipe can staff. */
  size: z.number().int().min(1).max(16).optional(),
  name: z.string().min(1).optional(),
});
export const PutBandRequestSchema = z.object({ band: BandSchema });
export const BandListResponseSchema = z.object({ bands: z.array(BandSchema) });
export const BandResponseSchema = z.object({ band: BandSchema });
export const DeleteBandResponseSchema = z.object({ deleted: z.boolean() });

3. The Anthropic brain

Brain interface — .../mate/src/intelligence/brain/types.ts

// :5 — Something the brain wants done. The machine (never the brain) applies these to the ports.
export type Action =
  | { type: "createMidiTrack"; name?: string }
  | { type: "createClip"; track: number; slot: number; lengthBeats: number }
  | { type: "addNotes"; track: number; slot: number; notes: MidiNote[] }
  | { type: "setTempo"; bpm: number }
  | { type: "fireClip"; track: number; slot: number }
  | { type: "startPlayback" }
  | { type: "stopPlayback" }
  | { type: "loadDrumKit"; track: number; rackUri: string; kitPath: string }
  | { type: "splicePromptToStack"; prompt: string; bpm: number };

// :24
export interface Decision {
  /** What the bandmate says back to the drummer. */
  message: string;
  actions: Action[];
  /** A command the loop should enqueue after acting, optionally delayed. */
  followUp?: { cmd: CommandBody; delayMs?: number };
}

// :39
export interface BrainInput {
  snapshot: SessionState;
  goal?: string;
  userText?: string;      // text of the triggering userRequest
  history: HistoryEntry[];
  trigger: CommandType;
}

// :48
export interface Brain {
  readonly kind: "anthropic" | "scripted";
  decide(input: BrainInput, signal: AbortSignal): Promise&lt;Decision&gt;;
}

AnthropicBrain — .../mate/src/intelligence/brain/anthropic.ts

// :9
export interface AnthropicBrainOptions {
  client: Anthropic;
  model: string;
  log: Logger;
  ableton: AbletonPort;
  splice: SplicePort;
  maxIterations?: number;   // default 8
  maxTokens?: number;       // default 16000
}

Ports are injected because read-only tools run inline inside decide; mutating tools are collected as Actions and applied later by EffectRunner.

The SDK call (:47-59) — a manual multi-turn tool loop, not a one-shot:

const response = await client.beta.messages.create(
  {
    model,
    max_tokens: this.opts.maxTokens ?? 16000,
    system: SYSTEM_PROMPT,
    tools: this.tools,
    messages,
    // Server-side refusal fallbacks: route by refusal category without maintaining a model list.
    betas: ["server-side-fallback-2026-07-01"],
    fallbacks: "default",
  },
  { signal },
);

// :61
if (response.stop_reason === "refusal") {
  log.warn("brain refused", response.stop_details);
  return { message: "I can't help with that one, ...", actions };
}

Notes: beta.messages.create (not messages.create); no thinking param (CLAUDE.md: "adaptive thinking (omit thinking)"); the abort signal comes from EffectRunner's per-request AbortController; the refusal check happens before any content is read; loop exits when stop_reason !== "tool_use" or no tool_use blocks; final message falls back to "On it." / "Nothing to change right now.".

Tool dispatch per iteration (:78-98): read-only (get_session, splice_search) execute against the ports and return JSON as tool_result; schedule_follow_up becomes decision.followUp; everything else goes through toolToAction() and is answered with "queued as action #N" — the model is told explicitly in the system prompt that actions haven't happened yet within the turn.

Prompt shaping: compactSession(s) (:130) trims the snapshot to transport + tracks + filled slots only; renderInput(input) (:147) renders trigger/goal/user text/snapshot JSON/last 8 history lines, and appends a "this is a periodic check, reply briefly with no tool calls" hint for tick triggers.

Credential resolution — .../mate/src/intelligence/brain/index.ts:34-49

export async function createBrain(mode: BrainMode, opts: CreateBrainOptions): Promise&lt;BrainResult&gt; {
  if (mode === "scripted") return { brain: new ScriptedBrain(demoScript()), live: false };
  try {
    const client = new Anthropic();                        // env-based resolution
    if (!client.apiKey &amp;&amp; !client.authToken) {
      throw new Error("no Anthropic credentials found (ANTHROPIC_API_KEY, ANTHROPIC_AUTH_TOKEN, or an `ant auth login` profile)");
    }
    return { brain: new AnthropicBrain({ client, model: opts.model, ... }), live: true };
  } catch (err) {
    if (mode === "anthropic") throw new Error(`MATE_BRAIN=anthropic but the client could not be created: ${reason}`);
    opts.log.warn(`brain: falling back to scripted (${reason})`);
    return { brain: new ScriptedBrain(demoScript()), live: false, fallbackReason: reason };
  }
}

No key is ever passed explicitly; new Anthropic() resolves from env, then the code asserts apiKey || authToken. auto degrades to scripted with a reason; anthropic throws.

ScriptedBrain — .../mate/src/intelligence/brain/scripted.ts

Script = Decision[] | ((input, callIndex) =&gt; Decision | Promise&lt;Decision&gt;). Records every BrainInput in .calls, supports push(), rejectNext(err), hold()/release()/pendingCount() for concurrency tests, honours the abort signal, falls back to a default decision when the queue empties. This is what every offline test uses.

Is there a "call the LLM once and get structured JSON" helper?

No. Grep across mate/, app/, packages/ for tool_choice, output_format, response_format, json_schema, zodToJsonSchema, toJSONSchema returns zero hits. There is no forced-tool-use JSON extractor, no zod-validated model output, nothing that calls the API outside AnthropicBrain.decide.

Closest existing primitives to build on: - strict: true + additionalProperties: false hand-written JSON Schemas on every tool (intelligence/tools/ableton.tools.ts, splice.tools.ts) — the repo's established way of constraining model output. - The manual loop + refusal check in anthropic.ts:46-100. - DocumentStore's schema.safeParse pattern for validate-on-the-boundary.

bun.lock:65 pins @anthropic-ai/sdk@0.123.0 (deps json-schema-to-ts, optional peer zod ^3.25 || ^4, which hints at first-class zod/structured-output helpers), but node_modules/ is not installed here so I could not verify what the package actually exports. Before writing such a helper, load the claude-api skill rather than reasoning from memory — the code already uses betas: ["server-side-fallback-2026-07-01"] and fallbacks: "default", which are newer than recall.

Also note the repo pins zod ^3.23.8 in mate/package.json, and document-store.ts imports ZodTypeDef (a zod-3 type) — any helper must stay zod-3 compatible.

loadConfig(env = process.env) (:26) maps env → schema: MATE_PORT, MATE_CORS_ORIGIN, MATE_TICK_MS, MATE_ABLETON, MATE_SPLICE, MATE_BRAIN, MATE_MODEL, SPLICE_MCP_URL, SPLICE_MCP_TOKEN, ABLETON_MCP_COMMAND, ABLETON_MCP_ARGS (space-split), MATE_MAX_BRAIN_RETRIES, MATE_TEMPLATES_DIR, MATE_BANDS_DIR. Tests call loadConfig({}) to get pure defaults.

Adapter wiring — three parallel factories, all called in index.ts:29-49, all returning { port|brain, live, fallbackReason? }:

Mode Ableton (ports/ableton/index.ts:23) Splice (ports/splice/index.ts:22) Brain (brain/index.ts:34)
stub/scripted InMemoryAbletonAdapter FixtureSpliceAdapter ScriptedBrain(demoScript())
mcp/anthropic connect + getSnapshot(), else throw connect + listTools() (no credit-spending calls), else throw construct client + assert creds, else throw
auto try real, fall back to stub with fallbackReason same same → scripted

index.ts:51 then publishes the outcome via store.setAdapters({ ableton, splice, brain }), which is what GET /adapters and the adapters SSE event report.

Composition (:34-64): global cors({ origin: config.corsOrigin, allowMethods: ["GET","POST","DELETE","OPTIONS"], allowHeaders: ["Content-Type"] }) → app.onError logs and returns {error} 500 → seven sub-routers each app.route("/", ...) (each router is its own new Hono() returned from a factory taking only the deps it needs) → app.notFound returns {error: "no route for METHOD path"} 404. startServer uses Bun.serve({ port, fetch: app.fetch, idleTimeout: 0 }) — idleTimeout: 0 for the long-lived SSE stream.

Zod validation idiom (identical in every mutating route)

let raw: unknown;
try { raw = await c.req.json(); } catch { return c.json({ error: "invalid JSON body" }, 400); }
const parsed = XSchema.safeParse(raw);
if (!parsed.success) return c.json({ error: "invalid band", issues: parsed.error.issues }, 400);

The two /generate routes additionally tolerate a missing body: they only read JSON when content-type includes application/json, otherwise raw = {} (bands.ts:62-69). Path params are checked with isValidBandId / isValidTemplateId before touching the store (defence in depth — the store throws too). Generated documents are re-validated against the full schema and 500 if the generator produced something invalid.

SSE — routes/events.ts

streamSSE(c, async stream =&gt; {...}). Order matters: the bus subscription is installed before the first write so nothing emitted while the snapshot is in flight is lost (comment at :31); writes are queued so the snapshot still lands first. First frame is event: snapshot, data: JSON.stringify(store.snapshot()); thereafter every MateEvent is forwarded as event: &lt;event.type&gt;, data: JSON.stringify(event) with a monotonic id. : keepalive &lt;ts&gt; comment frame every 15 s. finish() is idempotent and wired to both stream.onAbort and c.req.raw.signal.

Emitters are StateStore (state.changed, phase.changed, goal.changed, message, command.received, adapters) and EffectRunner (action.applied, cancelled). Event union: packages/protocol/src/events.ts:6.

export interface Sound {            // :1
  uuid: string;
  fileName: string;
  bpm: number | null;
  durationSec: number | null;
  type: "loop" | "oneshot";
  pack: string;
  tags: string[];
  url: string;
}

export interface SearchOptions {    // :12   (this is the "SearchQuery" analogue)
  bpmMin?: number;
  bpmMax?: number;
  type?: "loop" | "oneshot";
}

export interface StackLayer { uuid: string; layerType: string; sound: Sound; }   // :18

export interface Stack {            // :24
  uuid: string;
  name: string;
  bpm: number;
  layers: StackLayer[];
  shareUrl?: string;
  /** Raw text from the server, kept because Splice returns markdown rather than JSON. */
  raw?: string;
}

export interface DownloadResult { uuid: string; fileName: string; url: string; }  // :34

export interface SplicePort {       // :40
  readonly kind: "mcp" | "stub";
  searchSounds(query: string, opts?: SearchOptions): Promise&lt;Sound[]&gt;;
  promptToStack(prompt: string, bpm: number): Promise&lt;Stack&gt;;
  createStack(seedUuid: string, bpm?: number): Promise&lt;Stack&gt;;
  /** Spends a Splice credit on first download of an asset; not wired to any brain tool yet. */
  downloadAsset(uuid: string): Promise&lt;DownloadResult&gt;;
  close(): Promise&lt;void&gt;;
}

export interface SplicePortResult { port: SplicePort; live: boolean; fallbackReason?: string; }

Note there is no Sample type — the domain noun is Sound, and the multi-layer bundle is Stack.

Stub (stub.ts): FixtureSpliceAdapter loads fixtures/sounds.json (10 real Sounds, e.g. TS_ORGANIC_VOL1_124_drum_loop_... bpm 124, pack "That Sound", tags ["drums","live sounds","acoustic","rock","fills","pop"]). searchSounds tokenises the query (words > 2 chars), filters by type/bpm range, scores by substring hits across fileName + pack + tags, and always returns at least 3 results so the brain has something to work with. promptToStack/createStack synthesise a deterministic 4-layer stack keyed by hashHex(key) with layer types ["drums","percussion","bass","keys"] and a fake but well-formed UUID. createStack throws if the seed is a one-shot. Records every call in .calls.

MCP adapter (mcp.ts): McpSpliceAdapter wraps McpConnection({kind:"http", url, bearerToken}, log, "aibleton-mate-splice"). Tool name mapping — searchSounds → describe_a_sound (args query, bpm_min, bpm_max, type), promptToStack → prompt_to_stack (appends " at N bpm" when the prompt doesn't mention bpm, and sends a ±10 bpm window), createStack → create_stack (asset_uuid), downloadAsset → download_asset (logs a warning that it may spend a credit). Every response is markdown, parsed by markdown.ts (parseSearchResults, parseStack, parseDownload). Auth is an acknowledged open question in the class doc comment: mate has no Splice OAuth, so auto normally falls back to the fixture stub.

The domain types themselves live in packages/protocol/src/state.ts (re-exported by the port):

Arrangement support is read-only today: ArrangementClip appears in the snapshot and is counted in compactSession (arrangementClips: t.arrangementClips.length), but no Action, tool, or port method writes to the arrangement — everything mutating targets session clip slots.

snapshot.schema.ts handles the snake_case↔camelCase boundary: lenient .passthrough() raw schemas with defaults, parseSnapshotV2, toSessionState(raw, capturedAt), and kind = t.is_midi_track ? "midi" : "audio".

8. Existing notions of song / section / template / arrangement / bars / key / bpm / genre

Form and sections — packages/protocol/src/form.ts:

SectionLabelSchema  // :4  /^[a-z]$/ — single lowercase letter
export const FormEntrySchema = z.object({ label: SectionLabelSchema, bars: z.number().int().positive() });  // :14
export const DEFAULT_SECTION_BARS = 8;   // :21
parseForm(form, defaultBars = 8): FormEntry[]   // :34  "a8 b8 a8 b8 b8 c4 c4 b8 b8", commas ok, bare letters take defaultBars

formLabels(entries): SectionLabel[]             // :52  distinct, first-appearance order
formTotalBars(entries): number                  // :64
FormStringSchema                                // :69  z.string().superRefine(parseForm) — use at API boundaries

Length lives on the occurrence, not the section, so b can be 8 bars in one place and 4 in another.

Template (the "song roadmap") — packages/protocol/src/templates.ts:

export const SectionSchema = z.object({          // :8
  label: SectionLabelSchema,
  brief: z.string().min(1),                      // prompt handed to Splice
  /** Reserved for a later energy/tension input (e.g. a DAW plugin). Nothing reads it yet. */
  intensity: z.number().min(0).max(1).optional(),
});
const TemplateShape = z.object({                 // :16
  id: z.string().min(1),
  name: z.string().min(1),
  /** Form string, e.g. "a8 b8 a8 b8 b8 c4 c4 b8 b8". */
  form: FormStringSchema,
  sections: z.record(z.string(), SectionSchema),
  bpm: z.number().positive().optional(),
  createdAt: z.number(),
});
export const TemplateSchema = TemplateShape.superRefine(...)  // :31 every form label must have a section, filed under its own label

Composition (template × band → the closest thing to a rendered "song") — same file, :60-90:

export const CompositionLayerSchema = z.object({ layerType, soundUuid, localPath: z.string().nullable() });
export const CompositionStackSchema = z.object({ stackUuid, layers: [...] });
/** A template bound to real Splice material, persisted so re-rendering costs no credits. */
export const CompositionSchema = z.object({
  id, templateId, bpm: z.number().positive(),
  /** Section label -&gt; the material chosen for it. */
  stacks: z.record(z.string(), CompositionStackSchema),
  layout: z.array(z.object({ label: SectionLabelSchema, startBeat: z.number().min(0), lengthBeats: z.number().positive() })),
  createdAt: z.number(),
});

Composition is entirely unwired — grep across mate/src, app/src and the rest of packages/protocol finds Composition only in this file. No store, no route, no compositionsDir in config.ts, no .mate/compositions/, no test. It is a declared contract waiting for an implementation, and it is the natural place for a "render a song" feature to land.

Song form generator — mate/src/core/generator.ts:60 generateForm(opts): a wandering walk that opens on a, introduces letters strictly in order and at most one per step, never sits on a label more than maxRun occurrences, and keeps returning to home (weights HOME 5 / FIRST 2.5 / NEW 2 / OTHER 1.5, repeats damped ×0.6). defaultSections(labels) (:153) assigns briefs by position — groove / lift / breakdown / "variation N".

bpm appears in six unreconciled places, with no single source of truth: Transport.tempo (live Ableton), Template.bpm? (optional roadmap tempo), Composition.bpm (required), Stack.bpm, Sound.bpm | null, and the setTempo action / set_tempo tool. Nothing converts bars → beats → seconds anywhere except Composition.layout's startBeat/lengthBeats fields, which nothing populates.

genre: GENRES (8 values) is a generation input only — the doc comment at bands.ts:4 is explicit that a saved band's genre lives in free-text metadata.genre, surfaced via bandGenre(). Templates have no genre field.

key (musical key / scale / root note): does not exist anywhere. No key, scale, root, mode, chord, or transpose concept in mate/src, packages/protocol, or the tool schemas. The only pitch information in the system is raw MIDI note numbers (0-127) and the drum-rack pitch legend in the system prompt (36 kick, 38 snare, 42/46 hats, 49 crash, 51 ride). Anything key-aware is greenfield.

arrangement: read-only, as described in §7.

16 files, 2171 lines, all bun test, no network (CLAUDE.md rule). Naming pairs with source: &lt;module&gt;.test.ts, plus &lt;feature&gt;.store.test.ts / &lt;feature&gt;.api.test.ts for the document-backed features.

Representative style — mate/test/loop.test.ts:1-40 (the harness() pattern):

import { describe, expect, test } from "bun:test";
import { ManualClock } from "../src/core/clock.ts";
import { EventBus } from "../src/core/events.ts";
import { Mailbox } from "../src/core/mailbox.ts";
import { StateStore } from "../src/core/state.ts";
import { silentLogger } from "../src/log.ts";
import { ScriptedBrain } from "../src/intelligence/brain/scripted.ts";
import { createIntelligence } from "../src/intelligence/index.ts";
import { FakeAbleton, FakeSplice } from "./helpers/fakes.ts";

function harness(script: Decision[] | ScriptedBrain = [], tickMs = 0) {
  const clock = new ManualClock(1_000);
  const events = new EventBus();
  const store = new StateStore(events);
  const mailbox = new Mailbox();
  const ableton = new FakeAbleton();
  const splice = new FakeSplice();
  const brain = script instanceof ScriptedBrain ? script : new ScriptedBrain(script);
  const loop = createIntelligence({ clock, mailbox, store, brain, ableton, splice, log: silentLogger,
                                    options: { tickMs, retryBaseMs: 100 } });
  loop.start();
  return { clock, events, store, mailbox, ableton, splice, brain, loop };
}

Conventions visible throughout: relative imports carry explicit .ts; ManualClock(1_000) and tickMs: 0 disable ambient timers; await loop.settle() instead of sleeping; assertions read the recording fakes (h.ableton.callsOf("setTempo")) and the event history (h.events.ofType("message")).

API test style — bands.api.test.ts:16-40: a hand-written idleIntelligence: Intelligence literal (six no-op methods), stores pointed at mkdtemp() dirs cleaned in afterEach, config: loadConfig({}), log: silentLogger, now: () =&gt; clock.now(), and requests driven through app.request(path, {method, headers, body}) — no server is bound. Responses are validated against the protocol schema (BandResponseSchema.parse(...)), which asserts the route and the contract in one step.

Store test style — bands.store.test.ts: mkdtemp(join(tmpdir(), "mate-bands-")) in beforeEach, rm(..., {recursive:true, force:true}) in afterEach, test.each tables for id validation, plus explicit adversarial cases — corrupt JSON, wrong-shape JSON, schema-invalid JSON, and traversal ids ("../evil", "/etc/passwd") asserted not to write outside the dir.

Bonus: the copyable "new document feature" recipe

Bands were added in five commits (79c2c87 store → 255ad54 generator → 8b7a14e routes → 832a2bd app page → e6d647b naming refinement), following the template feature exactly. If the parent is adding a sibling (e.g. Composition), this is the established path:

  1. Protocol first — packages/protocol/src/&lt;feature&gt;.ts: entity schema (superRefine for cross-field rules), derived type X = z.infer&lt;&gt; + type XDraft = z.input&lt;&gt;, small pure helpers. Add request/response schemas to packages/protocol/src/api.ts (Put*Request, Generate*Request, *ListResponse, *Response, Delete*Response). Export from packages/protocol/src/index.ts.
  2. Store — mate/src/core/&lt;feature&gt;s.ts: a ~30-line class XStore extends DocumentStore&lt;X&gt; plus isValidXId. Nothing else needed; DocumentStore handles dirs, validation, sorting, traversal safety.
  3. Generator (optional) — mate/src/core/&lt;feature&gt;-generator.ts: pure, seeded, mulberry32, throws on bad options, returns the generated fields only and leaves id/name/createdAt to the route. Note: mulberry32 is currently copy-pasted in core/generator.ts:33 and core/band-generator.ts:43 — a third copy is the moment to extract core/rng.ts.
  4. Routes — mate/src/api/routes/&lt;feature&gt;s.ts: export function xRoutes(deps: { x: XStore; now: () =&gt; number }): Hono, five routes, the try/catch json + safeParse + issues idiom, newId("prefix"), generate returns unsaved.
  5. Wiring — add a &lt;feature&gt;Dir to config.ts (+ MATE_&lt;FEATURE&gt;_DIR), a field to AppDeps and an app.route("/", ...) in server.ts, and construct the store in index.ts.
  6. Tests — &lt;feature&gt;.store.test.ts (mkdtemp/rm, corrupt + traversal cases) and &lt;feature&gt;.api.test.ts (idleIntelligence, ManualClock, app.request, validate responses with the protocol schema). Existing api.test.ts and bands.api.test.ts construct createApp with all stores, so adding a field to AppDeps requires touching both.

If the new feature needs the LLM (e.g. LLM-generated section briefs), the offline-test rule forces a Brain-shaped seam: inject the dependency and substitute a ScriptedBrain-style fake in tests, exactly as intelligence/effects.ts does — do not call the SDK from a core/ module. 13817021336751

19	const SYSTEM_PROMPT = `You are "mate", an AI bandmate sitting in on a drummer's practice session. You control an Ableton Live set on their machine and can browse Splice for sounds.
20	
21	How you work:
22	- You are not realtime. Each time you are called you get a fresh snapshot of the Ableton session (transport, tracks, clip slots), the drummer's current goal if any, what they just said if anything, and a short history of previous decisions.
23	- Read-only tools (get_session, splice_search) run immediately and return real data.
24	- Every other tool queues an action; actions are applied in order after you finish, and you will see the result next time. Do not assume an action has happened yet within the same turn.
25	- Drum rack pitches for add_notes: 36 kick, 38 snare, 42 closed hat, 46 open hat, 49 crash, 51 ride. Times are in beats; a bar of 4/4 is 4 beats.
26	- Keep tempo changes musical (usually 40-220 BPM) and prefer small, reversible edits: a click, a bass or keys loop to play along with, a groove reference clip.
27	- If you want to check back on the drummer later (for example after they practise a pattern for a few minutes), call schedule_follow_up.
28	
29	Your final message is spoken to the drummer. Keep it short, concrete and friendly: what you set up and what to try. No markdown headers.`;
30	
31	/** Brain backed by the Anthropic API. Manual tool loop; mutating tools become queued Actions. */
32	export class AnthropicBrain implements Brain {
33	  readonly kind = "anthropic" as const;
34	  private readonly tools = toolDefinitions();
35	
36	  constructor(private readonly opts: AnthropicBrainOptions) {}
37	
38	  async decide(input: BrainInput, signal: AbortSignal): Promise<Decision> {
39	    const { client, model, log } = this.opts;
40	    const maxIterations = this.opts.maxIterations ?? 8;
41	    const messages: Anthropic.Beta.BetaMessageParam[] = [{ role: "user", content: renderInput(input) }];
42	    const actions: Action[] = [];
43	    let followUp: Decision["followUp"];
44	    let lastText = "";
45	
46	    for (let i = 0; i < maxIterations; i++) {
47	      const response = await client.beta.messages.create(
48	        {
49	          model,
50	          max_tokens: this.opts.maxTokens ?? 16000,
51	          system: SYSTEM_PROMPT,
52	          tools: this.tools,
53	          messages,
54	          // Server-side refusal fallbacks: route by refusal category without maintaining a model list.
55	          betas: ["server-side-fallback-2026-07-01"],
56	          fallbacks: "default",
57	        },
58	        { signal },
59	      );
60	
61	      if (response.stop_reason === "refusal") {
62	        log.warn("brain refused", response.stop_details);
63	        return { message: "I can't help with that one, but I'm happy to set up something else to play along with.", actions };
64	      }
65	
66	      const text = response.content
67	        .filter((b): b is Anthropic.Beta.BetaTextBlock => b.type === "text")
68	        .map((b) => b.text)
69	        .join("\n")
70	        .trim();
71	      if (text) lastText = text;
72	
73	      const toolUses = response.content.filter((b): b is Anthropic.Beta.BetaToolUseBlock => b.type === "tool_use");
74	      if (response.stop_reason !== "tool_use" || toolUses.length === 0) break;
75	
76	      messages.push({ role: "assistant", content: response.content });
77	      const results: Anthropic.Beta.BetaToolResultBlockParam[] = [];
78	      for (const use of toolUses) {
79	        const args = (use.input ?? {}) as Record<string, unknown>;
80	        try {
81	          if (isReadOnlyTool(use.name)) {
82	            results.push({ type: "tool_result", tool_use_id: use.id, content: await this.runReadOnly(use.name, args) });
83	          } else if (use.name === "schedule_follow_up") {
84	            followUp = { cmd: { type: "userRequest", text: String(args.reason ?? "check in") }, delayMs: Number(args.delay_ms) || 0 };
85	            results.push({ type: "tool_result", tool_use_id: use.id, content: "scheduled" });
86	          } else {
87	            const action = toolToAction(use.name, args);
88	            if (!action) {
89	              results.push({ type: "tool_result", tool_use_id: use.id, content: `unknown tool ${use.name}`, is_error: true });
90	              continue;
91	            }
92	            actions.push(action);
93	            results.push({ type: "tool_result", tool_use_id: use.id, content: `queued as action #${actions.length}` });
94	          }
95	        } catch (err) {
96	          results.push({ type: "tool_result", tool_use_id: use.id, content: err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err), is_error: true });
97	        }
98	      }
99	      messages.push({ role: "user", content: results });
100	    }
101	
102	    const decision: Decision = {
103	      message: lastText || (actions.length ? "On it." : "Nothing to change right now."),
104	      actions,
105	    };
106	    if (followUp) decision.followUp = followUp;
107	    return decision;
108	  }
109	
110	  private async runReadOnly(name: string, args: Record<string, unknown>): Promise<string> {
111	    if (name === "get_session") return JSON.stringify(compactSession(await this.opts.ableton.getSnapshot()));
112	    if (name === "splice_search") {
113	      const sounds = await this.opts.splice.searchSounds(String(args.query ?? ""), {
114	        bpmMin: numOrUndefined(args.bpm_min),
115	        bpmMax: numOrUndefined(args.bpm_max),
116	        type: args.type === "oneshot" ? "oneshot" : args.type === "loop" ? "loop" : undefined,
117	      });
118	      return JSON.stringify(sounds.slice(0, 10));
119	    }
120	    throw new Error(`not a read-only tool: ${name}`);
121	  }
122	}
123	
124	function numOrUndefined(v: unknown): number | undefined {
125	  const n = Number(v);
126	  return Number.isFinite(n) && v !== undefined && v !== null && v !== "" ? n : undefined;
127	}

129	/** Trim the session to what the model needs: transport, tracks, filled slots. */
130	export function compactSession(s: SessionState) {
131	  return {
132	    transport: s.transport,
133	    tracks: s.tracks.map((t) => ({
134	      index: t.index,
135	      name: t.name,
136	      kind: t.kind,
137	      mute: t.mute,
138	      solo: t.solo,
139	      arm: t.arm,
140	      slotCount: t.clipSlots.length,
141	      clips: t.clipSlots.filter((c) => c.clip).map((c) => ({ slot: c.index, name: c.clip!.name, length: c.clip!.length, playing: c.clip!.isPlaying ?? false })),
142	      arrangementClips: t.arrangementClips.length,
143	    })),
144	  };
145	}

147	export function renderInput(input: BrainInput): string {
148	  const parts: string[] = [];
149	  parts.push(`Trigger: ${input.trigger}`);
150	  if (input.goal) parts.push(`Practice goal: ${input.goal}`);
151	  if (input.userText) parts.push(`Drummer says: ${input.userText}`);
152	  parts.push(`Session snapshot:\n${JSON.stringify(compactSession(input.snapshot))}`);
153	  if (input.history.length) {
154	    const lines = input.history.slice(-8).map((h) => {
155	      const what = h.command.type === "userRequest" ? `user: ${h.command.text}` : h.command.type;
156	      const did = h.decision ? `-> ${h.decision.actions.map((a) => a.type).join(", ") || "no actions"}; said: ${h.decision.message.slice(0, 120)}` : "";
157	      const res = h.results ? ` [${h.results.filter((r) => r.ok).length}/${h.results.length} ok]` : "";
158	      return `- ${what} ${did}${res}`;
159	    });
160	    parts.push(`Recent history:\n${lines.join("\n")}`);
161	  }
162	  if (input.trigger === "tick") parts.push("This is a periodic check. If nothing needs doing, reply briefly with no tool calls.");
163	  return parts.join("\n\n");
164	}

32	/** mulberry32: tiny, fast, good enough for song shapes. Pure — no `Math.random()`. */
33	function mulberry32(seed: number): () => number {
34	  let a = (seed | 0) >>> 0;
35	  return () => {
36	    a = (a + 0x6d2b79f5) >>> 0;
37	    let t = a;
38	    t = Math.imul(t ^ (t >>> 15), t | 1);
39	    t ^= t + Math.imul(t ^ (t >>> 7), t | 61);
40	    return ((t ^ (t >>> 14)) >>> 0) / 4294967296;
41	  };
42	}
43	
44	function requireInt(value: number, name: string, min: number, max?: number): void {
45	  if (!Number.isInteger(value)) throw new Error(`${name} must be a whole number, got ${value}`);
46	  if (value < min) throw new Error(`${name} must be at least ${min}, got ${value}`);
47	  if (max !== undefined && value > max) throw new Error(`${name} must be at most ${max}, got ${value}`);
48	}
49	
50	/**
51	 * Generate a song form string from a seed.
52	 *
53	 * The shape is a wandering walk that keeps falling back to `home`: it opens on
54	 * `a`, introduces letters strictly in order and never more than one per step,
55	 * and refuses to sit on any label for more than `maxRun` occurrences in a row.
56	 *
57	 * @example generateForm({ seed: 42 }) // "a8 b8 b8 c8 b8 a8 b8 a8 b8"
58	 * @throws if the options are out of range or `home` is not a letter in play.
59	 */
60	export function generateForm(opts: GenerateFormOptions): string {
61	  const alphabet = opts.alphabet ?? 3;
62	  const count = opts.count ?? 9;
63	  const maxRun = opts.maxRun ?? 2;
64	  const bars = opts.bars ?? DEFAULT_SECTION_BARS;
65	
66	  if (!Number.isInteger(opts.seed)) throw new Error(`seed must be a whole number, got ${opts.seed}`);
67	  requireInt(alphabet, "alphabet", MIN_ALPHABET, MAX_ALPHABET);
68	  requireInt(count, "count", 1);
69	  requireInt(maxRun, "maxRun", 1);
70	  requireInt(bars, "bars", 1);
71	
72	  const letters = ALPHABET.slice(0, alphabet);
73	  const home = opts.home ?? letters[1]!;
74	  if (!letters.includes(home as (typeof ALPHABET)[number])) {
75	    throw new Error(`home ${JSON.stringify(home)} is not one of the letters in play (${letters.join(", ")})`);
76	  }
77	
78	  const rng = mulberry32(opts.seed);
79	  const labels: string[] = [letters[0]!];
80	  let introduced = 1;
81	  let run = 1;
82	
83	  for (let i = 1; i < count; i++) {
84	    const last = labels[i - 1]!;
85	    const stepsLeft = count - i;
86	    const missing = alphabet - introduced;
87	
88	    // Introduce the next letter as late as possible, but never so late that the
89	    // remaining steps cannot fit the letters still owed. A brand new letter can
90	    // never break a run, so this is always a legal pick.
91	    if (missing >= stepsLeft) {
92	      const next = letters[introduced]!;
93	      introduced++;
94	      labels.push(next);
95	      run = 1;
96	      continue;
97	    }
98	
99	    const candidates: { label: string; weight: number }[] = [];
100	    const pool = missing > 0 ? letters.slice(0, introduced + 1) : letters.slice(0, introduced);
101	    for (const label of pool) {
102	      // Never exceed maxRun. The pool always holds another option: with one
103	      // letter introduced the next new letter is available, and with two or
104	      // more some other introduced letter is.
105	      if (label === last && run >= maxRun) continue;
106	      const isNew = label === letters[introduced];
107	      let weight = WEIGHT_OTHER;
108	      if (label === home) weight = WEIGHT_HOME;
109	      else if (isNew) weight = WEIGHT_NEW;
110	      else if (label === letters[0]) weight = WEIGHT_FIRST;
111	      if (label === last) weight *= REPEAT_FACTOR;
112	      candidates.push({ label, weight });
113	    }
114	
115	    const total = candidates.reduce((n, c) => n + c.weight, 0);
116	    let pick = candidates[candidates.length - 1]!.label;
117	    let roll = rng() * total;
118	    for (const candidate of candidates) {
119	      roll -= candidate.weight;
120	      if (roll < 0) {
121	        pick = candidate.label;
122	        break;
123	      }
124	    }
125	
126	    if (pick === letters[introduced]) introduced++;
127	    run = pick === last ? run + 1 : 1;
128	    labels.push(pick);
129	  }
130	
131	  const entries: FormEntry[] = labels.map((label) => ({ label, bars }));
132	  return stringifyForm(entries);
133	}
134	
135	function briefFor(index: number): string {
136	  switch (index) {
137	    case 0:
138	      return "main groove: the core beat and bassline, the song's foundation";
139	    case 1:
140	      return "lift: chorus energy, brighter and busier than the groove";
141	    case 2:
142	      return "breakdown: stripped back and darker, space where the groove was";
143	    default:
144	      return `variation ${index - 2} on the groove: same feel, new texture`;
145	  }
146	}
147	
148	/**
149	 * Starter sections for a set of labels, one per label, filed under its own
150	 * letter. Roles follow position: groove, lift, breakdown, then variations.
151	 * `intensity` is left unset — that field is reserved and nothing reads it.
152	 */
153	export function defaultSections(labels: readonly string[]): Record<string, Section> {
154	  const sections: Record<string, Section> = {};
155	  let index = 0;
156	  for (const label of labels) {
157	    if (sections[label]) continue;
158	    sections[label] = { label, brief: briefFor(index) };
159	    index++;
160	  }
161	  return sections;
162	}

33	  });

35	
36	  const spliceResult = await createSplicePort(config.splice, {
37	    url: config.spliceMcpUrl,
38	    token: config.spliceMcpToken,
39	    log: createLogger("splice"),
40	  });
41	  if (spliceResult.fallbackReason) log.warn(`splice: using stub (${spliceResult.fallbackReason})`);
42	
43	  const brainResult = await createBrain(config.brain, {
44	    model: config.model,
45	    log: createLogger("brain"),
46	    ableton: abletonResult.port,
47	    splice: spliceResult.port,
48	  });
49	  if (brainResult.fallbackReason) log.warn(`brain: using scripted (${brainResult.fallbackReason})`);
50	
51	  store.setAdapters({
52	    ableton: abletonResult.port.kind,
53	    splice: spliceResult.port.kind,
54	    brain: brainResult.brain.kind,
55	  });
56

63	    splice: spliceResult.port,

65	    options: { tickMs: config.tickMs, maxBrainRetries: config.maxBrainRetries },
66	  });
67	  intelligence.start();
68	
69	  const ticks = new TickSource(intelligence, clock);
70	  ticks.start();
71	  const midi = new NoopMidiSource(intelligence);
72	  await midi.start();
73	
74	  const app = createApp({
75	    store,
76	    templates,
77	    bands,
78	    intelligence,
79	    config,
80	    log: createLogger("api"),
81	    startedAt,
82	    mailboxSize: () => mailbox.size(),
83	    now: () => clock.now(),
84	  });
85	  const server = startServer(app, config.port, log);
86	
87	  log.info(
88	    `mate up | ableton=${abletonResult.port.kind} splice=${spliceResult.port.kind} brain=${brainResult.brain.kind} model=${config.model} tick=${config.tickMs}ms cors=${config.corsOrigin}`,
89	  );
90	
91	  let shuttingDown = false;
92	  const shutdown = async (signal: string) => {
93	    if (shuttingDown) return;
94	    shuttingDown = true;
95	    log.info(`${signal} received, shutting down`);
96	    ticks.stop();
97	    await midi.stop();
98	    try {
99	      await intelligence.stop();
100	    } catch (err) {
101	      log.warn("error stopping intelligence", err);
102	    }
103	    await Promise.allSettled([abletonResult.port.close(), spliceResult.port.close()]);
104	    server.stop(true);
105	    log.info("bye");
106	    process.exit(0);
107	  };
108	  process.on("SIGINT", () => void shutdown("SIGINT"));
109	  process.on("SIGTERM", () => void shutdown("SIGTERM"));
110	}
111	
112	main().catch((err) => {
113	  console.error("mate failed to start", err);
114	  process.exit(1);
115	});

13	  ableton: AbletonPort;

15	}

18	  brain: Brain;
19	  live: boolean;

21	}
22	
23	/** Demo script used when no LLM is available: greets, then answers every call without acting. */
24	export function demoScript(): Decision[] {
25	  return [
26	    {
27	      message:
28	        "Hey, I'm mate. I'm running without an LLM right now, so I'll just watch the session. Set MATE_BRAIN=anthropic with credentials to let me play along.",
29	      actions: [],
30	    },
31	  ];
32	}
33	
34	export async function createBrain(mode: BrainMode, opts: CreateBrainOptions): Promise<BrainResult> {
35	  if (mode === "scripted") return { brain: new ScriptedBrain(demoScript()), live: false };
36	
37	  try {
38	    const client = new Anthropic();
39	    if (!client.apiKey && !client.authToken) {
40	      throw new Error("no Anthropic credentials found (ANTHROPIC_API_KEY, ANTHROPIC_AUTH_TOKEN, or an `ant auth login` profile)");
41	    }
42	    return { brain: new AnthropicBrain({ client, model: opts.model, log: opts.log, ableton: opts.ableton, splice: opts.splice }), live: true };
43	  } catch (err) {
44	    const reason = err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err);
45	    if (mode === "anthropic") throw new Error(`MATE_BRAIN=anthropic but the client could not be created: ${reason}`);
46	    opts.log.warn(`brain: falling back to scripted (${reason})`);
47	    return { brain: new ScriptedBrain(demoScript()), live: false, fallbackReason: reason };
48	  }
49	}

1	import { Hono } from "hono";
2	import {
3	  GenerateTemplateRequestSchema,
4	  formLabels,
5	  parseForm,
6	  PutTemplateRequestSchema,
7	  TemplateSchema,
8	  type DeleteTemplateResponse,
9	  type Template,
10	  type TemplateListResponse,
11	  type TemplateResponse,
12	} from "@aibleton/protocol";
13	import { newId } from "../../core/commands.ts";
14	import { defaultSections, generateForm } from "../../core/generator.ts";
15	import { isValidTemplateId, type TemplateStore } from "../../core/templates.ts";
16	
17	export interface TemplateRouteDeps {
18	  templates: TemplateStore;
19	  /** Injected so tests can drive createdAt and the default seed from a ManualClock. */
20	  now: () => number;
21	}
22	
23	export function templateRoutes(deps: TemplateRouteDeps): Hono {
24	  const r = new Hono();
25	
26	  r.get("/templates", async (c) => {
27	    const body: TemplateListResponse = { templates: await deps.templates.list() };
28	    return c.json(body);
29	  });
30	
31	  r.get("/templates/:id", async (c) => {
32	    const id = c.req.param("id");
33	    if (!isValidTemplateId(id)) return c.json({ error: `invalid template id ${JSON.stringify(id)}` }, 400);
34	    const template = await deps.templates.get(id);
35	    if (!template) return c.json({ error: `no template ${id}` }, 404);
36	    const body: TemplateResponse = { template };
37	    return c.json(body);
38	  });
39	
40	  r.post("/templates", async (c) => {
41	    let raw: unknown;
42	    try {
43	      raw = await c.req.json();
44	    } catch {
45	      return c.json({ error: "invalid JSON body" }, 400);
46	    }
47	    const parsed = PutTemplateRequestSchema.safeParse(raw);
48	    if (!parsed.success) return c.json({ error: "invalid template", issues: parsed.error.issues }, 400);
49	    const { template } = parsed.data;
50	    if (!isValidTemplateId(template.id)) {
51	      return c.json({ error: `invalid template id ${JSON.stringify(template.id)}` }, 400);
52	    }
53	    const body: TemplateResponse = { template: await deps.templates.save(template) };
54	    return c.json(body);
55	  });
56	
57	  r.delete("/templates/:id", async (c) => {
58	    const id = c.req.param("id");
59	    if (!isValidTemplateId(id)) return c.json({ error: `invalid template id ${JSON.stringify(id)}` }, 400);
60	    const body: DeleteTemplateResponse = { deleted: await deps.templates.delete(id) };
61	    return c.json(body);
62	  });
63	
64	  /** Generates a template and hands it back unsaved; POST /templates persists it. */
65	  r.post("/templates/generate", async (c) => {
66	    let raw: unknown = {};
67	    if (c.req.header("content-type")?.includes("application/json")) {
68	      try {
69	        raw = await c.req.json();
70	      } catch {
71	        return c.json({ error: "invalid JSON body" }, 400);
72	      }
73	    }
74	    const parsed = GenerateTemplateRequestSchema.safeParse(raw ?? {});
75	    if (!parsed.success) return c.json({ error: "invalid options", issues: parsed.error.issues }, 400);
76	    const opts = parsed.data;
77	    const at = deps.now();
78	    const seed = opts.seed ?? at;
79	
80	    let form: string;
81	    try {
82	      form = generateForm({
83	        seed,
84	        ...(opts.alphabet !== undefined ? { alphabet: opts.alphabet } : {}),
85	        ...(opts.count !== undefined ? { count: opts.count } : {}),
86	        ...(opts.home !== undefined ? { home: opts.home } : {}),
87	        ...(opts.maxRun !== undefined ? { maxRun: opts.maxRun } : {}),
88	        ...(opts.bars !== undefined ? { bars: opts.bars } : {}),
89	      });
90	    } catch (err) {
91	      return c.json({ error: err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err) }, 400);
92	    }
93	
94	    const draft: Template = {
95	      id: newId("tpl"),
96	      name: opts.name ?? `Form ${seed}`,
97	      form,
98	      sections: defaultSections(formLabels(parseForm(form))),
99	      ...(opts.bpm !== undefined ? { bpm: opts.bpm } : {}),
100	      createdAt: at,
101	    };
102	    const checked = TemplateSchema.safeParse(draft);
103	    if (!checked.success) {
104	      return c.json({ error: "generator produced an invalid template", issues: checked.error.issues }, 500);
105	    }
106	    const body: TemplateResponse = { template: checked.data };
107	    return c.json(body);
108	  });
109	
110	  return r;
111	}

export function spliceToolToAction(name: string, input: Record<string, unknown>): Action | null {
  if (name === "splice_prompt_to_stack") {
    return { type: "splicePromptToStack", prompt: String(input.prompt), bpm: Number(input.bpm) };
  }
  return null;
}
import { mkdir, readdir, unlink } from "node:fs/promises";
import { join } from "node:path";
import type { ZodType, ZodTypeDef } from "zod";

/** Ids become filenames, so they are restricted to characters that cannot escape the directory. */
const DOCUMENT_ID = /^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{1,64}$/;

/** True when `id` is safe to use as a document filename. */
export function isValidDocumentId(id: string): boolean {
  return DOCUMENT_ID.test(id);
}

function isNotFound(err: unknown): boolean {
  return typeof err === "object" && err !== null && (err as { code?: unknown }).code === "ENOENT";
}

/** Default ordering key: `createdAt` when it is a number, otherwise 0. */
function createdAtOf(doc: unknown): number {
  const at = (doc as { createdAt?: unknown }).createdAt;
  return typeof at === "number" ? at : 0;
}

export interface DocumentStoreOptions<T> {
  /** Directory holding one `<id>.json` per document. Created lazily. */
  dir: string;
  /**
   * Validates on the way in and on the way out. Typed as a plain `ZodType` so
   * schemas refined with `.superRefine` (i.e. `ZodEffects`) are accepted.
   */
  schema: ZodType<T, ZodTypeDef, unknown>;
  /** Pulls the id — and therefore the filename — out of a document. */
  idOf: (doc: T) => string;
  /** Noun used in id error messages, e.g. `"template"`. Defaults to `"document"`. */
  kind?: string;
  /** Numeric sort key; `list` returns the largest first. Defaults to `createdAt`. */
  sortKey?: (doc: T) => number;
}

/**
 * File-backed document storage: one JSON file per document.
import type { Brain, BrainInput, Decision } from "./types.ts";

export type Script = Decision[] | ((input: BrainInput, callIndex: number) => Decision | Promise<Decision>);

const DEFAULT_DECISION: Decision = {
  message: "I'm listening. Tell me what you want to work on and I'll set something up.",
  actions: [],
};

/**
 * Deterministic brain for tests and for running mate with no Anthropic credentials.
 * Pops decisions off a script, records every input, and can be told to fail or hang.
 */
export class ScriptedBrain implements Brain {
  readonly kind = "scripted" as const;
  readonly calls: BrainInput[] = [];
  private queue: Decision[] = [];
  private fn: ((input: BrainInput, callIndex: number) => Decision | Promise<Decision>) | null = null;
  private failNext: Error | null = null;
  private hangs: { resolve: () => void }[] = [];
  private holding = false;

  constructor(script: Script = [], private readonly fallback: Decision = DEFAULT_DECISION) {
    if (typeof script === "function") this.fn = script;
    else this.queue = [...script];
  }

  /** Add decisions to the end of the script. */
  push(...decisions: Decision[]): void {
    this.queue.push(...decisions);
  }

  /** The next decide() call rejects with this error. */
  rejectNext(error: Error = new Error("scripted brain failure")): void {
    this.failNext = error;
  }

  /** While holding, decide() waits until release() is called (or the signal aborts). */
  hold(): void {
    this.holding = true;
  }
  release(): void {
    this.holding = false;
    const waiting = this.hangs;
    this.hangs = [];
    for (const w of waiting) w.resolve();
  }
  pendingCount(): number {
    return this.hangs.length;
  }

  async decide(input: BrainInput, signal: AbortSignal): Promise<Decision> {
    const index = this.calls.length;
    this.calls.push(input);
    if (this.holding) {
      await new Promise<void>((resolve, reject) => {
        const entry = { resolve };
        this.hangs.push(entry);
        signal.addEventListener("abort", () => reject(new Error("aborted")), { once: true });
      });

import { FromSchema, JSONSchema } from 'json-schema-to-ts';
import { AutoParseableOutputFormat } from "../lib/parser.js";
type NoInfer<T> = T extends infer R ? R : never;
/**
 * Creates a JSON schema output format object from the given JSON schema.
 * If this is passed to the `.parse()` method then the response message will contain a
 * `.parsed_output` property that is the result of parsing the content with the given JSON schema.
 *
 * Note: When `transform` is enabled (the default), the schema is deep-cloned before transformation,
 * so the original schema object is not modified.
 */
export declare function jsonSchemaOutputFormat<const Schema extends Exclude<JSONSchema, boolean> & {
    type: 'object';
}>(jsonSchema: Schema, options?: {
    transform?: boolean;
}): AutoParseableOutputFormat<NoInfer<FromSchema<Schema>>>;
export {};
//# sourceMappingURL=json-schema.d.ts.map----
import { FromSchema, JSONSchema } from 'json-schema-to-ts';
import { Promisable, BetaRunnableTool, BetaToolRunContext } from "../../lib/tools/BetaRunnableTool.js";
import { BetaToolResultContentBlockParam } from "../../resources/beta.js";
import { AutoParseableBetaOutputFormat } from "../../lib/beta-parser.js";
type NoInfer<T> = T extends infer R ? R : never;
/**
 * Creates a Tool with a provided JSON schema that can be passed
 * to the `.toolRunner()` method. The schema is used to automatically validate
 * the input arguments for the tool.
 */
export declare function betaTool<const Schema extends Exclude<JSONSchema, boolean> & {
    type: 'object';
}>(options: {
    name: string;
    inputSchema: Schema;
    description: string;
    run: (args: NoInfer<FromSchema<Schema>>, context?: BetaToolRunContext) => Promisable<string | Array<BetaToolResultContentBlockParam>>;
    /**
     * Optional cleanup hook for tools that hold process-level resources (e.g. a
     * persistent shell). `client.beta.sessions.events.toolRunner` calls it once
     * when iteration ends.
     */
    close?: () => void | Promise<void>;
}): BetaRunnableTool<NoInfer<FromSchema<Schema>>>;
/**
 * Creates a JSON schema output format object from the given JSON schema.
 * If this is passed to the `.parse()` method then the response message will contain a
 * `.parsed_output` property that is the result of parsing the content with the given JSON schema.
 *
 */
export declare function betaJSONSchemaOutputFormat<const Schema extends Exclude<JSONSchema, boolean> & {
    type: 'object';
}>(jsonSchema: Schema, options?: {
    transform?: boolean;
}): AutoParseableBetaOutputFormat<NoInfer<FromSchema<Schema>>>;
export {};
//# sourceMappingURL=json-schema.d.ts.map----
export interface BetaOutputConfig {
    /**
     * All possible effort levels.
     */
    effort?: 'low' | 'medium' | 'high' | 'xhigh' | 'max' | null;
    /**
     * A schema to specify Claude's output format in responses. See

     */

Tool reply2026-09-04T14:33:21.244Z
58:**Claude Fable 5.1, Claude Mythos 5.1, and Mythos Preview reject forced tool use:** `{"type": "any"}` and `{"type": "tool", "name": ...}` return a 400 there (`tool_choice: type "tool" and "any" are not supported for this model.` - on `count_tokens` and Batches too). It is a model-specific restriction (Claude Fable 5 and Claude Opus 5 accept them). Use `{"type": "auto"}` and state the expectation in the prompt ("Use the get_weather tool to answer") - `strict: true` on the tool keeps the schema-valid-arguments guarantee `any` gave you - or structured outputs (`output_config.format`) when the forced call only existed to extract JSON. `auto` and `none` are unaffected; `disable_parallel_tool_use` with `auto` still means at most one call (the "exactly one" combination with `any`/`tool` is gone). Combining `tool_choice` `any` with `strict: true` applies only on models that support forced tool use. See `shared/model-migration.md` -> Migrating to Claude Fable 5.1 from Claude Fable 5.
59-
60----
61-
62-### Tool Runner vs Manual Loop
63-
64-**Tool Runner (Recommended):** The SDK's tool runner handles the agentic loop automatically - it calls the API, detects tool use requests, executes your tool functions, feeds results back to Claude, and repeats until Claude stops calling tools. Available in Python, TypeScript, Java, Go, Ruby, PHP, and C# SDKs (beta). The Python SDK also provides MCP conversion helpers (`anthropic.lib.tools.mcp`) to convert MCP tools, prompts, and resources for use with the tool runner - see `python/claude-api/tool-use.md` for details. **Default to the tool runner** for any custom-tool agent.
65-
66-**The tool runner is not a black box - "I need control" is rarely a reason to drop to the manual loop.** Each iteration yields the assistant message *before* the tools run and lets you intervene, so most "fine-grained control" needs are covered without hand-writing the loop:
67-
68-- **Human-in-the-loop approval / gating** - gate in the tool's run function (return a "user declined" result instead of executing), or inspect the tool call in the yielded message and override the pending request with `set_messages_params()` / `setMessagesParams()` / `append_messages()` / `pushMessages()` to allow or deny *before* the tool executes. The runner runs your function automatically only if you don't intervene.
69-- **Error interception** - inspect the tool result before it returns to Claude (`generate_tool_call_response()` / `generateToolResponse()`); stop early or handle it yourself.
70-- **Result modification** - mutate the tool result before it goes back (e.g. add `cache_control` for prompt caching, or transform the output).
--
486-### JSON Schema Limitations
487-
488-**Supported:**
489-
490-- Basic types: object, array, string, integer, number, boolean, null
491-- `enum`, `const`, `anyOf`, `allOf`, `$ref`/`$def`
492-- String formats: `date-time`, `time`, `date`, `duration`, `email`, `hostname`, `uri`, `ipv4`, `ipv6`, `uuid`
493-- `additionalProperties: false` (required for all objects)
494-
495-**Not supported:**
496-
497-- Recursive schemas
498-- Numerical constraints (`minimum`, `maximum`, `multipleOf`)
499-- String constraints (`minLength`, `maxLength`)
500-- Complex array constraints
501-- `additionalProperties` set to anything other than `false`
502-
503-The Python and TypeScript SDKs automatically handle unsupported constraints by removing them from the schema sent to the API and validating them client-side.
504-
505-### Important Notes
506-
507-- **First request latency**: New schemas incur a one-time compilation cost. Subsequent requests with the same schema use a 24-hour cache.
508-- **Refusals**: If Claude refuses for safety reasons (`stop_reason: "refusal"`), the output may not match your schema.
509-- **Token limits**: If `stop_reason: "max_tokens"`, output may be incomplete. Increase `max_tokens`.
510-- **Incompatible with**: Citations (returns 400 error), message prefilling.
511-- **Works with**: Batches API, streaming, token counting, extended thinking.
512-

1	"use client";

3	import { useState, type FormEvent } from "react";
4	import type { ExternalCommand, Phase } from "@aibleton/protocol";
5	
6	interface Props {
7	  phase: Phase;
8	  disabled: boolean;
9	  send: (command: ExternalCommand) => Promise<void>;
10	}
11	
12	export function CommandBar({ phase, disabled, send }: Props) {
13	  const [text, setText] = useState("");
14	  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
15	
16	  const fire = async (command: ExternalCommand) => {
17	    setBusy(true);
18	    try {
19	      await send(command);
20	    } catch {
21	      // surfaced through useMateState.lastError
22	    } finally {
23	      setBusy(false);
24	    }
25	  };
26	
27	  const onSubmit = async (e: FormEvent) => {
28	    e.preventDefault();
29	    const trimmed = text.trim();
30	    if (!trimmed) return;
31	    await fire({ type: "userRequest", text: trimmed });
32	    setText("");
33	  };
34	
35	  const locked = disabled || busy;
36	
37	  return (
38	    <form onSubmit={onSubmit} className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 rounded-md border border-line bg-panel px-3 py-2">
39	      <input
40	        className="min-w-[240px] flex-1 rounded-sm border border-line bg-panel-2 px-2.5 py-1.5 text-sm outline-none placeholder:text-muted/70 focus:border-accent"
41	        placeholder="Tell your bandmate something… e.g. “give me a 4-bar rock groove at 110”"
42	        value={text}
43	        onChange={(e) => setText(e.target.value)}
44	        disabled={locked}
45	      />
46	      <button type="submit" disabled={locked || !text.trim()} className={btn("bg-accent text-black")}>
47	        send
48	      </button>
49	      <span className="mx-1 h-5 w-px bg-line" />
50	      {phase === "paused" ? (
51	        <button type="button" disabled={locked} onClick={() => fire({ type: "resume" })} className={btn()}>
52	          resume
53	        </button>
54	      ) : (
55	        <button type="button" disabled={locked} onClick={() => fire({ type: "pause" })} className={btn()}>
56	          pause
57	        </button>
58	      )}
59	      <button
60	        type="button"
61	        disabled={locked || (phase !== "deciding" && phase !== "acting")}
62	        onClick={() => fire({ type: "cancel" })}
63	        className={btn()}
64	      >
65	        cancel
66	      </button>

68	        refresh
69	      </button>
70	    </form>
71	  );

11	/** Coarse wall clock for relative timestamps; 0 on the server so SSR and hydration agree. */
12	function useNow(intervalMs: number): number {
13	  return useSyncExternalStore(
14	    (onChange) => {
15	      const t = setInterval(onChange, intervalMs);
16	      return () => clearInterval(t);
17	    },
18	    () => Math.floor(Date.now() / intervalMs) * intervalMs,
19	    () => 0,
20	  );
21	}

Tool reply2026-09-04T14:34:59.437Z
1	"use client";
2	
3	import { useCallback, useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
4	import {
5	  MateEventSchema,
6	  StateResponseSchema,
7	  type ExternalCommand,
8	  type MateEvent,
9	  type StateResponse,
10	} from "@aibleton/protocol";
11	import { getState, mateUrl, postCommand } from "./mate";
12	
13	export type Connection = "connecting" | "open" | "error";
14	
15	export interface MateView {
16	  state: StateResponse | null;
17	  connection: Connection;
18	  lastError: string | null;
19	  send: (command: ExternalCommand) => Promise<void>;
20	}
21	
22	const EVENT_TYPES: MateEvent["type"][] = [
23	  "state.changed",
24	  "phase.changed",
25	  "command.received",
26	  "message",
27	  "cancelled",
28	  "action.applied",
29	  "adapters",
30	  "goal.changed",
31	];
32	
33	const RECENT_LIMIT = 50;
34	
35	function applyEvent(prev: StateResponse | null, event: MateEvent): StateResponse | null {
36	  if (!prev) return prev;
37	  switch (event.type) {
38	    case "state.changed":
39	      return { ...prev, session: event.session };
40	    case "phase.changed":
41	      return { ...prev, phase: event.phase, error: event.error ?? null };
42	    case "command.received":
43	      return {
44	        ...prev,
45	        recentCommands: [event.command, ...prev.recentCommands.filter((c) => c.id !== event.command.id)].slice(
46	          0,
47	          RECENT_LIMIT,
48	        ),
49	      };
50	    case "message":
51	      return { ...prev, lastMessage: event.text };
52	    case "adapters":
53	      return { ...prev, adapters: event.status };
54	    case "goal.changed":
55	      return { ...prev, goal: event.goal };
56	    case "cancelled":
57	    case "action.applied":
58	      return prev;
59	  }
60	}
61	
62	/**
63	 * Loads /state once, then follows /events over SSE. Re-renders only when an event arrives.
64	 * Reconnects with capped exponential backoff when the stream drops.
65	 */
66	export function useMateState(): MateView {
67	  const [state, setState] = useState<StateResponse | null>(null);
68	  const [connection, setConnection] = useState<Connection>("connecting");
69	  const [lastError, setLastError] = useState<string | null>(null);
70	  const retryRef = useRef(0);
71	
72	  useEffect(() => {
73	    let source: EventSource | null = null;
74	    let timer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null = null;
75	    let disposed = false;
76	
77	    const scheduleReconnect = () => {
78	      if (disposed || timer) return;
79	      const delay = Math.min(30_000, 1_000 * 2 ** retryRef.current);
80	      retryRef.current += 1;
81	      timer = setTimeout(() => {
82	        timer = null;
83	        void connect();
84	      }, delay);
85	    };
86	
87	    const connect = async () => {
88	      if (disposed) return;
89	      setConnection("connecting");
90	      try {
91	        const initial = await getState();
92	        if (disposed) return;
93	        setState(initial);
94	        setLastError(null);
95	      } catch (err) {
96	        if (disposed) return;
97	        setConnection("error");
98	        setLastError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err));
99	        scheduleReconnect();
100	        return;
101	      }
102	
103	      source = new EventSource(mateUrl("/events"));
104	      source.onopen = () => {
105	        retryRef.current = 0;
106	        setConnection("open");
107	        setLastError(null);
108	      };
109	      source.onerror = () => {
110	        source?.close();
111	        source = null;
112	        setConnection("error");
113	        scheduleReconnect();
114	      };
115	      source.addEventListener("snapshot", (e: MessageEvent<string>) => {
116	        const parsed = StateResponseSchema.safeParse(JSON.parse(e.data));
117	        if (parsed.success) setState(parsed.data);
118	      });
119	      for (const type of EVENT_TYPES) {
120	        source.addEventListener(type, (e: MessageEvent<string>) => {
121	          const parsed = MateEventSchema.safeParse(JSON.parse(e.data));
122	          if (parsed.success) setState((prev) => applyEvent(prev, parsed.data));
123	        });
124	      }
125	    };
126	
127	    void connect();
128	    return () => {
129	      disposed = true;
130	      if (timer) clearTimeout(timer);
131	      source?.close();
132	    };
133	  }, []);
134	
135	  const send = useCallback(async (command: ExternalCommand) => {
136	    try {
137	      await postCommand(command);
138	      setLastError(null);
139	    } catch (err) {
140	      setLastError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err));
141	      throw err;
142	    }
143	  }, []);
144	
145	  return { state, connection, lastError, send };
146	}
147
